## text/index.md 选型决策后续产出记录

**Editor retained-host consumer note(2026-07-04)**:在迁移完成前,retained-host 仍是 runtime text 的编辑器消费方。本轮 `paint_text/font.rs::runtime_text_style_for_face(...)` 将 runtime layout/shaping 使用的 family 从 requested/generic family 收敛为 retained cache 已解析 `runtime_family`,避免 DengXian/等线 raster 与 `system-ui` layout family 分叉；后续同日 `paint_template_nodes/render_command_conversion/style/text.rs::aligned_text_x(...)` 也先投影到同一 retained resolved family 再测量 Center/Right/Start/End 起点,避免命令对齐宽度与实际 glyph paint family 分叉。继续扫描后,Material Divider 横向 label gap、Chip label frame、Alert message、Avatar text、Badge root text 与 Badge overlay frame/text 都从 `chars()*font_size*ratio` 估宽切到 `measure_runtime_text_width(...)`,并分别保留原有 painted font size、padding、min-width、anchor 和 available-width clamp。非 Material 的 Dialog action 槽宽也从 `chars()*DIALOG_ACTION_CHAR_WIDTH + 20` 切到同一 runtime text measurement + 显式 padding/clip guard；按钮、列表、树行与 Asset Browser toolbar/tab 入口已确认不走字符数估宽。这不扩大 `fontdb`/`fontdue`/`swash` 隔离违例,也不添加控件局部字体策略。

**Editor retained-host font collection index propagation(2026-07-07)**:继续处理用户截图中“等线已生效但字符左右间距/渲染位置仍偏左或偏右”。复核发现 `fontdb::FaceInfo.index` 只用于选中系统字体,但 `fontdue::FontSettings` 与 `swash::FontRef::from_index(...)` 仍默认 face 0；对 `.ttc` 字体集合会造成 retained runtime family/name 与实际布局/栅格化 face 不一致,进而让 advance、bearing 与字形重心看起来不舒服。`paint_text/font.rs` 现在把 face index 存入 `HostTextFont.collection_index`,传给 fontdue `collection_index`,并写入 retained font cache key；`paint_text/raster.rs` 用同一 index 创建 swash `FontRef`；`font/tests.rs` 锁定 collection-index settings 与 cache-key 分离。该切片仍限制在 retained-host text owner 内,不新增 letter-spacing、控件局部字体、ZUI token、root painter、runtime FontDatabase 或 atlas route。direct editor test binary 运行 `retained_text_editor_crop_labels_keep_stable_ink_spacing` 通过 1/1,真实 framebuffer/full-label/narrow-label PNG 写入 `docs/tests/runtime/text`,SHA256 `8C81D6D27699ED503196F146636A3CF7EB51D202FF4E933AC96E6D1F17BD4E83` / `1C33579842EE9D0A912695219CDDA508BF247703151729C60A8EC93AD5365128` / `83B6CFDE5EAC92A9D2E349C605630484BC1FB5C3DA059F99D508D20B0E443339`;target/cargo-target 同名截图扫描为 0。当前机器导出的 proof 像素与 same-phase guard 一致,说明本机选中 face 很可能已是 index 0；本切片关闭的是非 0 collection face 的错面风险。focused Cargo wrapper 两次超时停在编译/警告阶段,不声明 Cargo green。

**Editor retained-host high-phase no-rollover clamp(2026-07-06, superseded)**:用户最新局部截图确认等线已生效后,nearest phase rollover 在 `editor base.zui` / `folder-op...line.svg` 这类小字号标签里仍会让接近 `.95px` 的 glyph origin 横向滚到下一整数像素,表现为字符左右落点不舒服。该历史切片曾恢复并收紧 no-rollover clamp:`paint_text/draw/placement/metrics.rs::screen_subpixel_bin(...)` 将 rounded 8-bin 结果 clamp 到 `RETAINED_TEXT_SUBPIXEL_BINS - 1`,`RetainedGlyphPlacement::from_screen_x(...)` 不再执行 `pixel_x + 1` rollover,因此 `20.95px`/`44.95px` 保留在当前 pixel cell 的 `0.875` phase。2026-07-07 复核后,该策略已由 nearest-phase quantization supersede。该切片只改 retained-host placement owner 与 tests,不改 ZUI token、组件局部字距、root painter、runtime FontDatabase、layout advance 或 atlas routing。focused Cargo `retained_glyph_placement` 通过 6/6,截图型 `retained_text_editor*` proof 通过 3/3,并刷新 latest/tiny crop PNG 与 4x 检查图到 `docs/tests/runtime/text`;unit log SHA256 `E23E53F2EAC1919738A080D8099E7C54CE6206A0B5D5CFD52DCF90FFEF5DBD69`,crop log SHA256 `73E4F4681610C1CFDF76742EB180515AC5F711E873AE95593E6D5A8E6E44208A`;target/cargo-target 同名截图扫描 0。

**Editor retained-host grayscale device-origin snap(2026-07-06)**:用户继续反馈等线小字号 label 的左右间距/渲染位置不舒服后,默认灰度路径的整行小数起点被重新收束。`paint_text/draw/placement.rs::retained_text_origin_for_smoothing(...)` 现在让 `HostTextSmoothing::Grayscale` 通过 `retained_text_origin_device_px(...)` snap 到 nearest device pixel,而显式 `HostTextSmoothing::Subpixel` 仍保留 finite fractional origin；per-glyph 1/8px alpha phase 继续生效,当前 high-phase 规则由 2026-07-07 nearest-phase quantization 接管。这个切片 supersede 2026-07-05 默认 grayscale fractional line-origin 结论,不改字体族、ZUI token、组件局部字距、root painter、runtime FontDatabase、layout advance 或 atlas routing。direct editor lib-test filters 通过 3/3,日志 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_grayscale_origin_snap_direct_binary_rerun_20260706.log` SHA256 `36023FD8988B93B9AD5B8D3E67768E4E5981CFEFB16805E75E5B901EB00C7864`;截图导出 focused tests 通过 2/2,日志 SHA256 `F29E93BFD991BA9A8EEF0688EFAB17C63F178CA8ECE1BD225F99CE554D5D4798`;真实 retained `HostRgbaFrame` proof PNG 位于 `docs/tests/runtime/text`,zoom4 full/narrow SHA256 `60927435F9EC63EE226B98C87F2D532AFA787A8A907AFDD008DF19EDEE73A228` / `237C4A1D2FD9350C2785FFDBC6949A1775E4627CEF47F63F215200115E812D9F`;target/cargo-target 同名 artifact 扫描 0。

**Runtime shared shaped-run provider routing + shape-count guard(2026-07-06)**:紧接 editor crop 的 retained-host修正后,PF-M1 继续把 runtime UI measurement 与 full layout 拉回同一 shaping 结果。`graphics/text/shaping/mod.rs` 新增 `TextShapeRunProvider`,line metrics、line-break、ellipsis、grapheme advances 与 source-range measure 均有 provider path；`UiTextMeasureCache` 现在持有 `ShapedRunCache`,natural-size measure miss 与 full-layout miss 通过同一个 `UiCachedTextShapeProvider` 取得 `Arc<ShapedGlyphRun>`。本轮继续修复 focused 验证链路:`render_segmented_controls.rs`、`render_selection_controls.rs`、`render_sliders.rs` 不再写已删除的 `UiStateFlags.focused/hovered`,而是通过 `UiSurface.component_states` 设置动态 focus/hover；`line_box.rs` 对不含 `\t` 的普通文本直接返回 provider-measured grapheme advances,不再为了 tab alignment 额外 shape `" "`,避免 `editor base.zui` 这类小标签在 measure/layout 间产生多余 shaped-run miss。新增 `render_perf_text_measure_then_layout_shapes_once` 作为首个 PF-M1 shape-count perf guard:先预热稳定 `"Hg"` line metrics,再断言 `editor base.zui` 的 measure+layout 只对真实 source label 产生 1 次 shaped-run miss/insert；这保留稳定行高样本,不把真实字符串行高引入换行容量。`text_measure_cache_reshapes_when_wrap_bucket_changes` 同步改为按实际 `measure_line_width(...)` 构造窄/宽 frame,避免不同平台字体度量让单词本身触发 glyph-wrap。`text_measure_cache_reuses_shaped_runs_between_measure_and_layout` 通过 1/1,日志 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_spacing_cache_layout_focused_test_20260706.log` SHA256 `1DCD85CCAA299AF05AEA2CDCEEA331023D408473CF297356B8AA6114F16DE40A`;本轮 WSL 直接运行 lib-test binary `render_perf_text_measure_then_layout_shapes_once` 通过 1/1,日志 SHA256 `15A8674D402A487BF3A833FC2C87FFB0799E7B316BC652E8E80D459F58319B42`,`text_measure_cache` 通过 9/9,日志 SHA256 `071AD21B834B93D1A314B4E6B06763ED0E5DD4EFB72258E164C1AD6169517C80`;现有 no-rollover crop 视觉证据仍在 `docs/tests/runtime/text`,本轮目检 zoom4 full/narrow label PNG SHA256 `154EAE7F1443E561F912BF82F57F25F922ED220F0EF3AD0B416ED67F8A706C29` / `095C3D75B8B1E981A86062614D9DCF47E4BD897BCF014EEC684D84C2DD70250F`;target/cargo-target 新增同名验证图扫描 0。

**Editor retained-host nearest phase rollover(2026-07-06, restored 2026-07-07)**:该历史尝试曾让 rounded bin 到达 8 时进入下一 pixel cell 的 `0.0` phase,把单个 glyph pen-origin 量化误差约束到 `<= 0.0625px`。2026-07-07 用户 crop 复核后,该核心策略已通过 full screen x nearest-phase quantization 恢复为当前有效实现,以避免 high-phase clamp 的系统性左右偏置。历史验收日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_nearest_phase_rollover_acceptance_20260706.log` SHA256 `619ECCF3EBD438CA8B70C16B1F99E88AA69F4357A037DBFC91A93390CDDB0FB7`。

**Editor retained-host system-ui fallback stack(2026-07-05)**:继续收窄最新 crop 中“等线已生效但字符左右间距/渲染位置仍偏左或偏右”的字体 identity 风险。`paint_text/font.rs` 现在让默认 `system-ui` / `ui-sans-serif` 先按 `DengXian`、本地化 `等线`、`Microsoft YaHei UI`、`Segoe UI` 查询,最后才回到 generic `Family::SansSerif`;显式 `sans-serif`/`monospace` 等 generic 名保持原有语义。这样 retained-host raster 与 runtime measure 的默认 UI face 不再完全交给 fontdb generic resolution,且不在组件、ZUI token、root painter 或字距表中硬编码局部策略。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_system_ui_fallback_stack_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `285C542709C091DBC29D7DB6C82BBEEBD627B4792B0DF8E9CE33E8F5AE9C25F0` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_system_ui_fallback_stack_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `453143764C1F4C7237AC821E6DA789926CD853A4F39BC255A9FDB7FF6334D1FF`;Cargo 编译日志 `runtime_text_editor_retained_system_ui_fallback_stack_cargo_test_20260705.log` SHA256 `CF682BDA40453E91ED4AFF7AE3434A9297B7404DB4DC33F34F60A3BF66375531`;target/cargo-target 同名扫描 0。scoped rustfmt 已通过；focused Cargo 在 904s Windows 编译窗口超时且无 Rust diagnostics/test summary,不计 green。

**Editor retained-host pen-origin raster-bearing correction(2026-07-05)**:最新截图中“等线已生效但字符左右间距/渲染位置仍偏左或偏右”的剩余 retained-host 问题还包括 2026-07-04 bitmap-left handoff 过度相信 `RuntimeTextGlyph.x`。本轮收窄该结论:`paint_text/draw/glyphs.rs` 正常绘制路径以 `origin_x` 对应的 layout pen origin 为 placement authority,再叠加当前 raster backend 的 `raster.metrics.x_offset` bearing；`RuntimeTextGlyph.x` 只作为 invalid-origin fallback。这样 shaped-origin、line-origin policy、resolved family 与 swash/fontdue 当前 raster bearing 不再互相覆盖,也不新增组件局部字体/字距策略。

**Editor retained-host layout bitmap-left authority reopen(2026-07-05)**:用户继续提供 editor crop 后,上方 pen-origin+raster-bearing 结论对 finite layout x 被重新收窄；该条现在作为历史状态保留,已被同日 `raster-bearing alignment reclose` 再次取代。当时临时规则是两段式: `origin_x` 只负责 retained raster phase/subpixel sample,有限 `RuntimeTextGlyph.x` 负责最终 bitmap-left draw pixel；只有 layout `glyph.x` 非 finite 时才用 `origin_x + raster.metrics.x_offset` fallback。`paint_text/draw/glyphs.rs` 与 `draw/glyphs/tests.rs` 曾按该规则更新,保持 ZUI 字体 token、root painter、runtime FontDatabase 和 atlas routing 不变。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_layout_bitmap_left_authority_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `1256315A761B5815D1E816607C8ACA16102C354FC3B461650BDEDF80497AF46D` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_layout_bitmap_left_authority_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `23AFB4FC698A2C5E305867AD1308D057E20AAF94999521F62FEC33E413A7AB90`;target/cargo-target 同名扫描 0。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred,live editor window typography QA 仍 open。

**Editor retained-host raster-bearing alignment reclose(2026-07-05)**:用户最新局部截图确认 layout bitmap-left authority 仍会让等线小字号标签出现字符左右偏移观感,因此当前生效规则恢复并收紧为: `RuntimeTextGlyph.origin_x` 是正常 placement authority,负责 retained 1/8px phase；最终 bitmap-left draw pixel 使用 `origin_pixel_x + raster.metrics.x_offset`,让 swash/fontdue 当前光栅 backend 的真实 bearing 决定字形边界；`RuntimeTextGlyph.x` 只在 `origin_x` 非 finite 时作为 layout fallback。`draw/glyphs/tests.rs` 新增 stale layout-left 场景,防止有限 layout x 再覆盖有效 pen origin。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_raster_bearing_alignment_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `7B66F057AA95412E65BDE04CF9908C42D47E3CD3598EE041622E23F5AB4A663B` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_raster_bearing_alignment_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `255DCCF5EA9B559595EB0A50BADF2A36C04BB1515894DF247977D38857A763C2`;focused Cargo `retained_text_editor_crop_labels_keep_stable_ink_spacing` 第三轮通过 1/1,并导出真实 framebuffer/crop PNG 到 `docs/tests/runtime/text`；target/cargo-target 同名扫描 0。live editor-window typography QA 仍 open。

**Editor Document Tab clip-guard centering(2026-07-05)**:最新截图中的 `editor base.zui`/`folder-op...line.svg` 还暴露了 Button 内容层问题:`text_clip_guard` 原本被加入 label measured width 后参与 icon+title content centering,导致实际文字墨迹相对 tab center 左偏或右偏半个 guard。`template_buttons/content/metrics.rs` 现在将 ink width 与 text slot width 分开,`template_buttons/content/entry.rs` 只用 icon+title ink width 居中,把 clip guard 留在 text command width；`template_buttons_tests/paint.rs` 用 `DockTab0` + `folder-open-outline` + `editor base.zui` 锁定 visual center。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_tab_clip_guard_centering_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `33B58720DB6662D1F6455EB5C40676239C5A8505AC5D8C8B13AA49E13CC6E476` / `runtime_text_editor_tab_clip_guard_centering_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `D0E62EAF9A757D0C6680FE3CB3F3E6799E0ADFBE396535478278EC4BE28A771D`;target/cargo-target 同名扫描 0。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred,live editor window typography QA 仍 open。

**Editor retained-host Fontdue bearing-fraction sampling(2026-07-05)**:同一最新截图的 fallback raster 残留问题继续收窄。前一切片已把正常 bitmap-left 放回 `origin_x + raster metrics.x_offset`,但 FontdueFallback 下采样仍只带 pen-origin phase,没有带高分辨率位图自己的 fractional bitmap-left bearing。`paint_text/raster.rs` 现在通过 `fontdue_fallback_sample_offset_x(...)` 合并 pen-origin phase 与 bitmap-left fraction;`draw/glyphs.rs`/`draw/glyphs/row.rs` 允许组合 sample offset 超过 1px,避免被 0.999 clamp 重新制造 floor bias。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_fontdue_bearing_fraction_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_fontdue_bearing_fraction_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 仍因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Editor retained-host grayscale alpha-phase correction(2026-07-05)**:用户继续指出等线字体已经生效后,字符左右间距仍像偏左/偏右。复核后确认上一轮“每个 grayscale glyph 都 nearest device-pixel snap”会把自然 fractional advance 压成不均匀的整像素步进,在 `editor base.zui` / `folder-open.svg` 小字号标签里产生 0.68px+ 的局部 advance 抖动。本轮将 `HostTextSmoothing::Grayscale` 的语义收窄为 swash `Format::Alpha` 覆盖格式,不再代表逐字整像素吸附；`retained_glyph_placement_for_smoothing(...)` 对 grayscale 与 explicit subpixel 都使用 `RetainedGlyphPlacement::from_screen_x(...)` 的 8-bin x 相位。随后 2026-07-06 device-origin snap 只把默认 Grayscale line origin 贴回 nearest device pixel,per-glyph Alpha coverage + 8-bin glyph phase 仍保留；显式 Subpixel line origin 继续 fractional。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_alpha_phase_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_alpha_phase_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Editor retained-host grayscale device-glyph placement(2026-07-08)**:用户最新局部 crop 继续显示默认灰度小字号等线文本的 glyph 左右落点仍有不适。当前默认 `HostTextSmoothing::Grayscale` 因此进一步收窄为 Alpha coverage + nearest device-pixel glyph placement:`RetainedGlyphPlacement::from_device_pixel_x(...)` 返回整数 pixel 与 `0.0` sample offset；explicit `HostTextSmoothing::Subpixel` 仍走完整 screen x 的 1/8px nearest-phase。layout bridge 的 smoothing-aware share-bin guard 同步按当前策略判断,所以默认灰度下跨 nearest device-pixel boundary 的 runtime/shaped projection 会 fail-closed 到 host natural spacing。该切片不新增 letter-spacing、控件局部字体、ZUI token、root painter、runtime FontDatabase 或 atlas route。静态验证日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_device_glyph_snap_rustfmt_20260708.log` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_device_glyph_snap_diff_check_20260708.log`;direct binary proof 生成 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_device_glyph_snap_20260708*.png`,full/narrow zoom4 已目检,focused Cargo wrapper 仍待独立 green。

**Editor retained-host grayscale layout guard(2026-07-05)**:上一轮新增的 `retained_glyph_placements_share_bin_for_smoothing(...)` 仍保留,但当前策略已随 alpha-phase correction 调整为 grayscale/subpixel 共用 8-bin glyph placement。`paint_text/draw/layout.rs::runtime_advances_preserve_retained_raster_bins(...)` 继续从 active `HostTextSmoothing` 读取策略,防止 runtime advance projection 与最终 glyph placement 分叉；现在 20.49→20.51 对 grayscale alpha 与 explicit subpixel 都会被视为同一 retained phase,避免逐字整像素 rounding boundary 把 compact label 拉出锯齿状间距。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_alpha_phase_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_alpha_phase_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Editor retained-host invalid-origin smoothing fallback(2026-07-05)**:继续关闭同一 smoothing policy 的漏口。`draw/glyphs.rs::retained_glyph_bitmap_pixel_x(...)` 在 `RuntimeTextGlyph.origin_x` 不可用时仍把 `glyph.x` 当 layout bitmap-left fallback,但不再直接调用固定 placement helper；draw path 已按当前 smoothing 计算 `placement.pixel_x`,invalid-origin fallback 复用该 pixel。当前 alpha-phase correction 后,默认 grayscale 与 explicit subpixel 在 `glyph.x=20.51` 时都落在 pixel 20 + 0.5 phase,只在 swash render format/coverage 解码上分流。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_alpha_phase_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_alpha_phase_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Editor retained-host subpixel line-origin preservation(2026-07-05)**:继续收束计划中显式 Subpixel/LCD background composite 的前置数据面。`layout_text_run(...)` 之前对 line origin 无条件调用 `retained_text_origin_device_px(...)`,导致即使 `HostTextSmoothing::Subpixel` 已保留 per-glyph 8-bin placement,整段 editor label 仍先被吸到整数 x。`draw/placement.rs` 提供 `retained_text_origin_for_smoothing(...)` 后,最初只让显式 subpixel 保留 finite fractional line origin；2026-07-05 曾短暂让默认 grayscale 也切到同一 finite fractional line-origin 策略,但 2026-07-06 已恢复默认 Grayscale device-origin snap。本条保留为 smoothing-aware owner 的历史前置证据。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_subpixel_line_origin_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_subpixel_line_origin_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Editor retained-host grayscale fractional line-origin(2026-07-05, superseded)**:该条现为历史尝试。默认 grayscale 改成 Alpha coverage + 8-bin glyph phase 后,当时判断整行起点继续 nearest-device-pixel snap 会把 `8.875px` 这类 editor tab/file label origin 推到 `9.0px`,因此曾让 Grayscale 与 Subpixel 都保留 finite fractional line origin。后续用户 crop 仍显示默认灰度小字号 label 有整段左右漂移感,所以 2026-07-06 grayscale device-origin snap 已 supersede 该默认灰度策略；显式 Subpixel 仍保留 fractional line origin。历史验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_fractional_origin_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_fractional_origin_validation_20260705.log`。

**Editor retained-host shaped-origin phase fallback(2026-07-05)**:用户最新 crop 继续显示同一等线小字号标签的字符左右落点不舒服。复核后确认 runtime shaped positions 虽然 glyph id、source range 与局部 advance 匹配,仍可能把某个 glyph 的 pen origin 推过 retained 1/8px raster phase 边界,导致 `editor base.zui` / `folder-open.svg` 这类 compact label 看起来某些字符偏左或偏右。本轮在 `paint_text/draw/layout.rs` 的 shaped-position 接收路径恢复 phase guard,但只拒绝跨 retained phase 的 shaped origin；同相位的 shaped position 仍继续使用 runtime pen origin。新增 `runtime_positioned_glyphs_rejects_matching_shaped_positions_that_shift_raster_phase` 覆盖该 fallback。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/editor_text_retained_phase_guard_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `D3B66322AB0997F9FF16AA80A88D8CF589C6B3C40954FDF9A0FD475979035D83` / `editor_text_retained_phase_guard_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `BDF87532D019E57FB7DEAB83F69EC49AEF72480B18EC847FC3F5DF982615AF95`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Editor retained-host same-phase origin drift guard(2026-07-07)**:用户最新局部截图说明同一 retained 1/8px phase 内仍可能出现肉眼可感的左右落点不适。2026-07-05 的 same-phase 接收规则因此被收窄: `paint_text/draw/layout/metrics.rs::glyph_origin_matches_without_visible_drift(...)` 现在把 host natural origin 与 shaped/runtime candidate origin 的差值限制在 `0.03125px`; `shaped_positions_preserve_retained_raster_bins(...)` 与 `runtime_advances_preserve_retained_raster_bins(...)` 同时要求 finite、低于可见漂移门槛、且仍共享 retained placement bin。`draw/layout/tests.rs` 保留 `0.02px` 级合法 same-phase nudge,并新增 `runtime_positioned_glyphs_reject_matching_shaped_positions_with_same_phase_visible_drift` 覆盖 `0.04px~0.05px` 同相位但可见的漂移回退。本切片仍只修 retained-host layout bridge,不改字体族、ZUI asset、root painter、runtime FontDatabase、glyph atlas identity 或组件局部 letter-spacing。验证日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_same_phase_origin_drift_guard_validation_20260707.log` SHA256 `7193440149FA3C4FB4CC384E0905769C93D398050968D1B0349899946943FA36`;direct editor test binary 运行 same-phase proof 通过 1/1,日志 SHA256 `542818B5FF18C3ABADE5A1A8713F11062C16CCFBE3B019728CF1886EDAD06ED5`;真实 retained framebuffer/full-label/narrow-label PNG 已写入 `docs/tests/runtime/text`,SHA256 `8C81D6D27699ED503196F146636A3CF7EB51D202FF4E933AC96E6D1F17BD4E83` / `1C33579842EE9D0A912695219CDDA508BF247703151729C60A8EC93AD5365128` / `83B6CFDE5EAC92A9D2E349C605630484BC1FB5C3DA059F99D508D20B0E443339`;target/cargo-target 同名截图扫描 0。focused Cargo proof 仍无 `test result`,不声明 Cargo green;live editor-window typography QA 仍 open。

**Editor retained-host same-phase proof stem hook(2026-07-07)**:为补齐上一个切片的 fresh proof PNG,`paint_text_tests.rs::export_editor_crop_framebuffer_if_requested()` 新增 `ZR_TEXT_EDITOR_CROP_PROOF_STEM`。`ZR_TEXT_EDITOR_CROP_PROOF_DIR` 仍是唯一写文件开关,默认 stem 继续保留 20260705 crop artifact 名称；设置 stem 后可把 framebuffer、full-label crop、narrow-label crop 与 log 写成 same-phase-origin-drift 专属文件,避免覆盖已验收的旧 crop 证据。该切片只服务真实 framebuffer proof 归档,不是纯文本策略目标截图,也不改变布局、字体、ZUI、root painter、runtime FontDatabase 或 atlas 路径。验证日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_same_phase_origin_drift_guard_proof_stem_hook_validation_20260707.log` SHA256 `A7936DC9ADD771338B9852F6FEC819821DD1334630E65247C6609E039A6D4F48`;focused Cargo proof 首次尝试 exit `-1` 停在 early compile(日志 SHA256 `F48EA3A4BC40D50D354BCD6B1FD7BFBCA7BF353180DBA37A6DF6A2F92969DF36`),rerun 在 923s 工具窗口内停于编译阶段且无 `test result`(日志 SHA256 `D4BF6A0E76223C687EECE892AE8456301FAECA2695426BD24D47850C6DA834DB`),rerun2 在 480s 工具窗口内仍停于 compile/warnings 且无 `test result`(日志 SHA256 `6471B3A85089FD72C3B8EC752306C4860A60E428E469FBC97DEF0731EBFE2F3A`);随后 direct editor test binary 使用 same stem 写出 PNG/log 到 `docs/tests/runtime/text`,acceptance log SHA256 `B9BB847E9E8EB857F24619B2BDC40F71D7782D47E738BC05FDA141A2D230AF4D`;target/cargo-target 同名截图扫描为 0(scan SHA256 `39736FD9FC4F493629257F0F73F6CFD8CD129D120A955ABA3E0E0D6F3D0E9B95`)。

**Editor retained-host crop framebuffer export hook(2026-07-05)**:同一 editor crop 问题已经有 `retained_text_editor_crop_labels_keep_stable_ink_spacing` 的 framebuffer 像素断言,但缺少按需导出真实 `HostRgbaFrame` PNG 的测试路径。本轮在 `paint_text_tests.rs` 增加 `ZR_TEXT_EDITOR_CROP_PROOF_DIR` 环境变量钩子,默认不写文件；设置后导出 `runtime_text_editor_retained_crop_framebuffer_20260705.png/.log` 到调用方指定目录,用于后续把用户截图同引擎 framebuffer 输出对齐。2026-07-05 第三轮 focused Cargo 已通过 1/1,真实 PNG/日志写入 `docs/tests/runtime/text`,汇总证据见 `runtime_text_editor_retained_crop_export_cargo_passed_20260705.log`。

**Editor retained-host crop framebuffer region guard(2026-07-05)**:继续针对用户 crop 中“等线已生效但字符左右间距/渲染位置仍偏左或偏右”的 proof gap。导出钩子已经能生成完整 retained-host `HostRgbaFrame`,但 test 断言仍主要扫描小尺寸 fixture,没有直接覆盖 proof framebuffer 中和截图相似的 full/narrow label 区域。本轮让 `retained_text_editor_crop_labels_keep_stable_ink_spacing` 复用 `editor_crop_proof_framebuffer()`,分别扫描 `editor base.zui` 与窄 `folder-open.svg` proof region 的 painted pixels 和最大内部空列,并把 region profile 写入 opt-in export log。验证日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_crop_framebuffer_region_guard_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `27F709D3AE0B90C411C808A840D1AE79746613EC18152F72B5C82F8B1E780D2A`;复用 preview PNG SHA256 `CCDEB289958F388258466745699C7521B9DB4B72C700C2300B93C8FA5761B5BA`。这只是 retained framebuffer 区域守卫增强,不是 live editor-window QA 完成。

**Editor retained-host crop region export PNGs(2026-07-05)**:继续补齐同一 proof 链路的可查看输出。`paint_text_tests.rs::export_editor_crop_framebuffer_if_requested()` 现在在 `ZR_TEXT_EDITOR_CROP_PROOF_DIR` 设置时,除了完整 `runtime_text_editor_retained_crop_framebuffer_20260705.png`,还会从同一 `HostRgbaFrame` 裁出 `runtime_text_editor_retained_crop_full_label_20260705.png` 与 `runtime_text_editor_retained_crop_narrow_label_20260705.png`,并把三份 artifact path 写入 log。第三轮 focused Cargo 导出的真实 PNG 为 `runtime_text_editor_retained_crop_framebuffer_20260705.png` SHA256 `69B95BB5E3AAFDA2B586C785F5AAD9361452605D6594653DD2CA663AAF750073`,`runtime_text_editor_retained_crop_full_label_20260705.png` SHA256 `B4F1C117C182F89AB59C449E1C6A4B0EEBC338128190A63A52CE56D8423B4628`,`runtime_text_editor_retained_crop_narrow_label_20260705.png` SHA256 `8AEE9DCCC0ECE7AAD53D5FC9C42C7494694801430CEDBF399558064609E0A324`;target/cargo-target 同名扫描 0。live editor-window typography QA 仍 open。

**Editor retained-host SubpixelMask sample phase(2026-07-05)**:继续收窄同一小字号 editor label 左右相位问题的低层采样分叉。`paint_text/draw/glyphs/row.rs::sampled_subpixel_coverage(...)` 之前在 supersampled RGB coverage 下采样时忽略 `sample_offset_x`,导致 AlphaMask 已按 1/8px phase 采样而 SubpixelMask fallback/放大路径仍按未偏移窗口采样。本轮让 SubpixelMask 与 AlphaMask 共用 normalized horizontal sample offset math,并用 `sampled_subpixel_coverage_applies_fallback_phase` 锁定 offset `0.0` 与 `0.5` 会输出不同 RGB coverage。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_subpixel_sample_phase_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `359111D8D764B8239316A47B6D148AFD96492B8F263E9A30B7578F0A3FFE1C96` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_subpixel_sample_phase_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `DD3AEBCB6974787B447394704B78E444553F7B80AF0DF916F7CE63075A6258C6`。这关闭的是 retained RGB row sampler 的相位丢失漏洞;focused Cargo 与 live editor-window typography QA 仍 open。

**Native bitmap atlas TextArea bounds clipping(2026-07-05)**:`scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas.rs` 的 alpha source-feed 不再把 partial `TextArea.bounds` clip 作为整帧 glyphon fallback 条件。alpha glyph image 先按 glyphon bitmap pixel rect 建 source,再把 bounds intersection 转为 source-row crop,同步更新 `GlyphAtlasBitmapSource.screen_rect`、`content_size` 与 `source_byte_len`；完全在 bounds 外的 glyph 不进入 visible source 计数,Color/Subpixel glyph 在 bounds 内仍 fail-closed。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_bitmap_atlas_text_area_bounds_clip_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_bitmap_atlas_text_area_bounds_clip_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 仍因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Native bitmap atlas prepare report + storage guard(2026-07-05)**:`scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas.rs` 现在输出 `NativeBitmapAtlasPrepareReport`,把 visible/source/unsupported/clipped glyph 计数、submission counters、单一 `GlyphAtlasStorageFormat`、mixed-storage flag 与 `replaces_glyphon` 决策汇总到 `ScreenSpaceUiTextPrepareReport.native_bitmap_atlas`。当前 renderer 仍是单 texture-array storage,所以 `replaces_glyphon()` 必须确认同一帧没有混用 R8/RGBA storage；混合 AlphaMask+Color/Subpixel 的 submission 会显式保留 glyphon native path,不再有“误把 RGBA 送入 R8”的隐性风险。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_prepare_report_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_prepare_report_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 仍因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Native bitmap atlas RGBA source handoff(2026-07-05)**:`scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas.rs` 现在把 `SwashContent::Color` 映射到 `GlyphAtlasFormat::Color`,按 RGBA row stride 裁剪 source bytes,并把 Color source foreground multiplier 固定为 white,避免颜色 emoji/彩色字形被文本前景色二次染色。`SwashContent::SubpixelMask` 也会进入 `SubpixelMask` source/report；该切片当时仍由 `requires_background_composite` 阻止 glyphon replacement,后续 background input 与 replacement route 已只打开已知不透明背景场景。新增回归已拆到 `text/native_bitmap_atlas/tests.rs`,父 owner 只保留生产逻辑和测试挂载。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_rgba_source_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_rgba_source_validation_20260705.log`；focused Cargo 已尝试 `native_bitmap_atlas` filter,但 904s 后仍在 Windows lib-test 编译期超时,日志为 `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_rgba_source_cargo_test_20260705.log`,不计通过。

**Native bitmap atlas mixed-storage renderer cutover(2026-07-05)**:`scene_renderer/ui/text.rs` 新增 `NativeBitmapAtlasHandoff`,把 native bitmap atlas replacement 决策显式拆为 single-storage replacement、mixed-storage replacement 和 glyphon fallback。`NativeBitmapAtlasPrepareReport.mixed_storage_replacement_ready` 为 true 时,contiguous R8/RGBA frame 现在通过 `NativeBitmapAtlasFrame::storage_submissions()` 生成 per-storage submission,再交给 `GlyphAtlasBitmapRenderer::prepare_storage_submissions(...)` 写入独立 texture-array storage pass；该路径会关闭 glyphon render 并 trim glyphon `TextAtlas`。非 contiguous storage order 和 `requires_background_composite` 仍 fail-closed 到 glyphon,避免 draw-order 或 Subpixel 背景合成半切换。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_mixed_storage_renderer_cutover_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_mixed_storage_renderer_cutover_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Native bitmap atlas retry-frame state execution(2026-07-05)**:`ScreenSpaceUiTextBackend` 现在真正持有 `GlyphAtlasBitmapRetryFrameState`,并把它传入 native bitmap atlas frame-loop。新增 `scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas/retry_frame.rs` 子 owner,只把当前帧仍可见的 blocked source 送入 retry frame driver,按 retry-aware input 顺序重排 source bytes,并丢弃不再可见的 stale blocked source；非零 visible blocked source 的 source bytes 顺序也由回归锁定。字体面失效和空文本帧会清空 retry state,避免旧 font/source 在后续帧被重试绘制。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_retry_frame_state_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `4E4F6035CE84D6501DCF272D59F50156193D608EC1C3BFCE7365AEE4A8071041` / `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_retry_frame_state_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `87B2631FE1AC2C87433F548FF97D75FF0AE75979BD4590B7F39F99B1E5C983AB`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Native bitmap atlas tests owner split(2026-07-06)**:按结构约定继续收束 `scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas/tests.rs`。父文件现在只保留 fixture 与 `#[path]` child mounts,source-cache idle/invalidation、handoff/fallback reason、source projection/cropping/format、frame replacement/storage/background-composite 回归分别进入 `tests/source_cache.rs`、`tests/handoff.rs`、`tests/source.rs`、`tests/frame.rs`,既有 `tests/retry_frame.rs` 保留 retry owner。该切片只移动测试块,不改生产 native atlas route、renderer handoff、glyphon fallback、source bytes、atlas upload 或截图输出；scoped rustfmt/diff check 通过,外部 cargo lanes 活跃时不启动新 runtime Cargo。

**SubpixelMask background composite input owner(2026-07-05)**:`scene_renderer/ui/render.rs` 现在把 UI render command 自身的不透明 `background_color` 解析为 `ScreenSpaceUiTextBatch.background_color: Option<[f32;4]>`,透明/缺失背景保持 unknown,不使用 fallback 猜测。`scene_renderer/ui/text.rs` 通过 `NativeBitmapAtlasTextArea` 将该背景输入和 glyphon `TextArea` 配对传入 native bitmap atlas；`native_bitmap_atlas.rs` 对 SubpixelMask source 保留已知背景色,并在 `NativeBitmapAtlasPrepareReport` 中记录 `background_composite_glyph_count` 与 `missing_background_composite_glyph_count`。本切片只关闭背景输入数据面和诊断,当时 Subpixel replacement 仍 fail-closed 到 glyphon；下方 replacement route 已补上已知不透明背景的替换路径。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_subpixel_background_input_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_subpixel_background_input_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**SubpixelMask background composite replacement route(2026-07-05)**:在上一个背景输入 owner 之后,`atlas/shaders/glyph_atlas_sampling.wgsl::glyph_atlas_decode_subpixel_rgb_coverage(...)` 不再输出 coverage alpha,而是输出已用背景合成后的 RGB 与背景 alpha；`scene_renderer/ui/atlas_renderer/pipeline.rs` 对 `GlyphAtlasBlendMode::SubpixelBackgroundComposite` 改用 color/alpha `REPLACE`,避免把已合成的背景再 alpha blend 一次。`NativeBitmapAtlasPrepareReport.background_composite_replacement_ready` 区分“已知不透明背景可替换”和“缺失背景必须 glyphon fallback”,`text.rs` handoff regression 锁定 background-ready Subpixel replacement。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_subpixel_background_composite_replacement_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_subpixel_background_composite_replacement_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**SubpixelMask inherited opaque UI background owner(2026-07-05)**:在 command 自身背景输入之后,`scene_renderer/ui/render/background.rs` 新增 `ScreenSpaceUiBackgroundTracker`,只从前序不透明纯色 UI quad 记录 background candidate,并用后续透明背景、图片、文字或边框命令作为 blocker；`render.rs` 继续只做命令规划编排,在生成 `ScreenSpaceUiTextBatch` 时调用 `text_batch_background_color(...)`。文本自身有透明/无效 `background_color` 时仍优先保持 unknown,不借用前序背景；只有当前可见文本 frame 被前序 candidate 完整覆盖且没有后续 blocker 交叠时,才把背景色交给 native bitmap atlas 的 SubpixelMask composite route。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_ui_inherited_opaque_background_preview_20260705.png` / `runtime_text_ui_inherited_opaque_background_validation_20260705.log`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**SubpixelMask framebuffer background input(2026-07-05)**:继续补上背景输入链的空场景/清屏首段。`render.rs::framebuffer_background_color(...)` 只在两类确定场景返回背景:clear attachment 且 clear color 不透明,或 load-store UI overlay 下的空场景不透明 `preview.clear_color`。后一类必须满足 skybox disabled、无 mesh/sprite/particle sprite、无 visible overlay；否则保持 unknown,不伪造 framebuffer readback。`ScreenSpaceUiBackgroundTracker::with_framebuffer_background(...)` 把该 known background 作为 order 0 candidate,后续透明 UI blocker 仍可使文本批次 `background_color=None`。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_framebuffer_background_input_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `6B4EF6653EC5D0096B8A95A50999207423D4673F9BF98417B3E5662BD618CBBD` / `runtime_text_framebuffer_background_input_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `2F398524CF72FCA715F4A45ABECAFA2561B793481D2203599AE8BCEA78E3AC58`。focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**SubpixelMask framebuffer GPU particle guard(2026-07-06)**:上面的 load-store framebuffer-background 首段继续保持 conservative。`known_loaded_framebuffer_background_color(...)` 现在不只看 CPU particle sprite 列表,也会否决 particle emitters、previous sprites、bounds 和 `RenderParticleGpuFrameExtract` 的 alive/spawned counters；`screen_space_ui_loaded_framebuffer_background_rejects_gpu_particle_content` 锁定 GPU 粒子存在时文本批次不能继承 `preview.clear_color`。验证日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_framebuffer_background_gpu_particle_guard_validation_20260706.log` SHA256 `B906087B5FA073C33DD3F874D8B107A6070E34C68D5CF4735A7D07A09013E294`；新 PNG/focused Cargo 因 active cargo/rustc lanes deferred,既有视觉基线仍为 `runtime_text_framebuffer_background_input_preview_20260705.png`。

**Atlas page generation upload guard(2026-07-05)**:继续补 PF-M1/09 的代际竞态防线。`GlyphAtlasPageSpec` 现在携带 `generation`,同一 `GlyphAtlasPageKey` 被 LRU eviction/rebuild 复用时 generation 递增；`GlyphAtlasUploadCommand`、bitmap upload staging page 与 `GlyphAtlasBitmapTextureUploadRequest` 均贯穿 `page_generation`。`glyph_atlas_bitmap_texture_upload_request_plan_with_atlas(...)` 会用当前 `GlyphAtlasSet` 丢弃旧 generation request,`atlas_texture_upload/binding.rs` 也拒绝 staging/request 自身 generation 不一致,`atlas_renderer/renderer.rs` 将 stale drops 计入 upload failure aggregate,避免过期异步产物进入 `Queue::write_texture` 或被上层诊断隐藏。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_atlas_page_generation_guard_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `E71C524CC707D941D5CFB97C7E9C7BE05DD75C6F4D6F40337FF62C72CFC92580` / `runtime_text_atlas_page_generation_guard_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `22A87474D8352455BD1B138CFD3BECC78541A6626CD1D728D402DD5898E450B7`。focused Cargo wrapper 超时 904s,已停止本次 target-dir 残留进程；非本切片 Cargo lanes 仍活跃。

**Native bitmap atlas source cache(2026-07-05)**:继续补 PF-M1/AT-M3 的持久化 glyph source cache 淘汰闭环首段。`scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas/source_cache.rs` 新增 `NativeBitmapAtlasSourceCache`,按 `CacheKey` 缓存 swash `SwashImage` 的 content、bearing、尺寸与 source bytes,用小容量 LRU 测试锁定最久未使用项先淘汰。`ScreenSpaceUiTextBackend` 现在跨帧持有该 source cache,`native_bitmap_atlas_frame(...)` 先查 cache,miss 才调用 `get_image_uncached(...)`,并把 `hit_count/miss_count/insert_count/evicted_count/entry_count` 汇入 `NativeBitmapAtlasPrepareReport.source_cache`。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_source_cache_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `1E14BAC0D645C799F0C1B6CED7B3B688810D0DA906E8296C513E90D517D71B10` / `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_source_cache_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `53D4E8BA41AF8F37023D61F52C054AB374AB358CF61AB7DE049995DA2F9993EE`。scoped rustfmt 已通过；focused Cargo 因非本切片 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Native bitmap atlas idle source-cache discard(2026-07-05)**:继续补 PF-M1/AT-M3 的显式 cache trim/flush 语义。前一切片建立了持久 source cache 与容量 LRU,但 native text 输入为空时 `ScreenSpaceUiTextBackend` 只 trim glyphon `TextAtlas`,不会释放 swash source bytes。`source_cache.rs::discard_all_for_idle_frame()` 现在在空 native frame 清空 source entries 并报告 `evicted_count/entry_count`;`native_bitmap_atlas_idle_prepare_report(...)` 将该报告写入 `NativeBitmapAtlasPrepareReport`;`text.rs` 的 `texts.is_empty()` 分支返回该报告并继续准备空 bitmap plan。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_idle_source_cache_discard_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `3AAEDFD539282C7B39145F58AA5FCCAD885B3D1056F79C89BEEE350A86845569` / `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_idle_source_cache_discard_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `01897DB95CFA33920506EBD15C514BF7398B2C13C5C588DE9AC5C43C69421C33`。scoped rustfmt 已通过；focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Native bitmap atlas face-invalidation source-cache guard(2026-07-05)**:继续针对用户截图中“字体已是等线但字符左右间距/落点仍不舒服”的 runtime native cache 生命周期风险补首段。`NativeBitmapAtlasSourceCacheFrameReport` 新增 `invalidated_count`,并由 `discard_all_for_face_invalidation()` 在字体资产集合变化时立即清空旧 swash source images,下一帧通过 `evicted_count/invalidated_count/entry_count` 暴露清理结果。`ScreenSpaceUiTextSystem::prepare(...)` 记录 auto text font 解析是否插入新资产,`ScreenSpaceUiTextBackend::prepare(...)` 也检查 explicit native text 是否在 prepare 中加载新资产,两条路径都在构造 native bitmap atlas frame 前失效 source cache。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_face_invalidation_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `6C3CFB3E81034048E1249EF64A014FD93F431DFDBE890AB1CB4DD22776B9EE84` / `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_face_invalidation_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `A2D021877470EC579A7FFAB9D10C64FB09C7EF59D1A8CB1FFCDD1E06C80D4C67`。scoped rustfmt 已通过；focused Cargo 因 6 个外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Native bitmap atlas renderer face-invalidation storage-pass telemetry(2026-07-05)**:继续补用户截图同源的 face/cache 生命周期诊断盲区。`GlyphAtlasBitmapRenderer` 现在在 face invalidation 时清空 active storage-pass draw/upload state,并在下一次 prepare report 中暴露 `invalidated_storage_pass_count`；`ScreenSpaceUiTextBackend` 同步清理 source cache、retry queue 与 renderer storage passes,避免旧 face atlas draw state 在字体资产变化后继续参与绘制。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_bitmap_renderer_face_invalidation_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `5D35B7421413F7C8B1C47E4AAC5B25794D8BC2DFB27CC66730732091EF981CB1` / `runtime_text_bitmap_renderer_face_invalidation_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `15B149B884E73979E2B16CBE3B6A2D94735362B3F7E086E8FBD5CE12CD9E0FF1`。target/cargo-target 同名扫描为 0；focused Cargo 因外部 lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Native bitmap atlas nearest sampler(2026-07-05)**:继续处理最新 editor crop 中“等线已生效但字符左右间距/渲染位置偏左或偏右”的 atlas sampling 风险。`atlas_renderer/resources.rs` 将 runtime bitmap atlas sampler 从 Linear min/mag 改为 Nearest min/mag/mipmap 并固定 LOD 0,与 glyphon bitmap cache 的 nearest sampling contract 对齐；`atlas_renderer/tests.rs` 锁定该 descriptor,避免线性过滤把相邻 atlas texel 混进小字号文件名标签左右边缘。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_bitmap_atlas_nearest_sampler_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `A8C071C64D89F6380CAC2D11B64970CD078051B9DD030AD3D1515395EF5C9A0B` / `runtime_text_bitmap_atlas_nearest_sampler_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `8734A1008CB635E072FFE89546368A579BAD4075DBEBABB6969031E747394DD0`。target/cargo-target 同名扫描为 0；2026-07-06 focused Cargo `glyph_atlas_bitmap_sampler_matches_glyphon_nearest_sampling_contract` 通过 1/1,日志 `runtime_text_bitmap_atlas_nearest_sampler_focused_cargo_20260706.log` SHA256 `AD2A6E83F4D73F08C1A53404740E1148353D791F0F15AE9316B783FAE4BE5692`,exit SHA256 `A9F58776A09B5DAC438049683F24BF85764E0FF8E7455952456165C68C158627`。

**Native bitmap atlas handoff owner(2026-07-05)**:继续按 `engine-code-structure-convention.md` 收束 text root 的混合职责。`scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas/handoff.rs` 现在承接 `NativeBitmapAtlasHandoff` 与 `native_bitmap_atlas_handoff_for_report(...)`;`scene_renderer/ui/text.rs` 只在 prepare frame 中消费 handoff 决策,不再同时拥有 root orchestration 与 glyphon/native cutover policy。原 handoff 回归迁入 `text/native_bitmap_atlas/tests.rs`,证明图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_handoff_owner_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `B97D06F24B38594DCECF485FEC38D27E825565D6AD9F48699476C22081901BDF` / `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_handoff_owner_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `B64F35B3D2B3785602012ED91FF60550CA05FD0B85E0FC2A91D82B5B3AA223D9`。该切片不改变渲染行为,但为后续完整 `TextAtlas` cutover/fallback reason telemetry 提供独立 owner；focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Native bitmap atlas fallback reason telemetry(2026-07-05)**:继续推进上面的 handoff owner 后续项。`NativeBitmapAtlasPrepareReport` 现在包含 `glyphon_fallback_reason`;`scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas/handoff.rs` 根据 report 字段按优先级推导 glyphon fallback 原因,使 `replaces_glyphon=false` 能细分为无可见字形、unsupported glyph format、source 覆盖缺口、LCD background composite 输入缺失、atlas allocation failure、mixed storage split 未就绪等。`text.rs` 仍只消费 handoff branch,没有新增 root policy。证明图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_fallback_reason_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `3B6A5965753EF9769E5CBCDAA1827F3EBF0A6A04C8D389260CF1B00CB65BB153` / `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_fallback_reason_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `B541E92E405B52A5A8D66E79EB3BCB5E3159422EF3EDD88FFD45AA09D228C09C`。target/cargo-target 同名扫描为 0；focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Native bitmap atlas frame index(2026-07-05)**:继续补 PF-M1/AT-M3 的生产 native bitmap atlas frame-loop 前置条件。`ScreenSpaceUiTextBackend` 现在持有 `bitmap_atlas_frame_index` 并在非空 native atlas frame 前单调递增,`native_bitmap_atlas_frame(...)`、`NativeBitmapAtlasPrepareReport.frame_index` 与 per-storage atlas submissions 统一消费该 live frame index。旧生产常量 `BITMAP_ATLAS_FRAME_INDEX = 1` 已移除,避免 blocked retry/page-residency telemetry 永远停在 frame 1 -> retry 2。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_frame_index_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `1C6259F792FC3A17AA45C2C4E454593EC16A46881E7B7856BC736FCEC1F84FB7` / `runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_frame_index_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `537F88AD32DA89317F814D6CE643D278DB742F1000C0C7891DDD180E2FBDF566`。scoped rustfmt/diff/static scans 已通过；focused Cargo 因外部 cargo/rustc lanes 活跃 deferred。

**Editor retained-host same-style shaped cluster line guard(2026-07-05)**:继续处理用户最新 editor crop 中“等线已生效但字符左右间距/渲染位置仍偏左或偏右”的同源问题。`paint_template_nodes/render_command_conversion/text/commands/shaped.rs::push_shaped_text_commands(...)` 现在先通过 `uniform_cluster_text_style(...)` 判断 runless shaped line 的 visible clusters 是否同为一种 `UiTextRunPaintStyle`;同样式的 `folder-op` + `…` + `line.svg` 不再拆成多条 retained-host `HostPaintCommand::text`,而是保留整行 frame/text 进入一次 host text layout/raster。混合 plain/code/strong/emphasis 的 shaped clusters 仍按 cluster split,保留 2026-07-02 的样式语义。验证图/日志:`docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_same_style_cluster_line_preview_20260705.png` SHA256 `83EA90E14589E020780B9EF58CC20557E72961A5EB74988D9CB40637275FDACB` / `runtime_text_editor_retained_same_style_cluster_line_validation_20260705.log` SHA256 `1B548F90039400909A94BD88CAAF3B7C63CAA1F676C4B0014FB21202175B6463`。scoped rustfmt 已通过；focused Cargo 先因错误 feature 立即失败,改用 `zircon_editor` no-default-features 后 604s 超时且无 Rust diagnostics,不计通过；live editor-window typography QA 仍 open。

**Editor Document Tab runtime-measure note(2026-07-04)**:最新截图中的 `editor base.zui` 与 `folder-open-line.svg` 属于 Workbench Document Tab 标签,其不适感来自可视 tab 宽度与拖拽命中区仍残留字符数/ASCII 宽度估算。`workbench/document_tabs/metrics.rs` 现在只接收 retained-host runtime text 已测量的 title width,`chrome_template_projection/dock_header.rs` 用 `measure_runtime_text_width(..., DOCUMENT_TAB_TITLE_FONT_SIZE)` 投影可视 tab frame,`retained_host/tab_drag/tab_width.rs` 也复用同一测量和 preferred-width helper,从而关闭 label paint、close hitbox 与 drag midpoint 的宽度分叉。

**Editor Host Page Tab runtime-measure note(2026-07-04)**:同一类文件名/页面名左右间距问题还存在于顶部 Host Page Tab owner。`workbench/page_tabs/metrics.rs` 现在移除 `TITLE_WIDTH_PER_CHAR`,只接收 runtime measured title width；`chrome_template_projection.rs` 用 `measure_runtime_text_width(..., MAIN_PAGE_TAB_TITLE_FONT_SIZE)` 分配可视 page tab frame；`retained_host/host_page_pointer/tab_strip_geometry.rs` 通过 `HostPagePointerItem.title` 复用同一测量分配共享 hitbox。这样顶部主页面页签的 paint frame、overflow 判定和 pointer route 不再分别依赖字符数或均分宽度。

**Editor Asset Browser file-name runtime-compaction note(2026-07-04)**:同一截图问题还存在于 Asset Browser 文件名截断 owner。`asset_browser/name_compaction.rs` 现在作为共享 file-like display-name compaction owner,由 table、thumbnail 和 selected-summary 节点先调用 `measure_runtime_text_width(...)` 判断完整名称是否能放入固定 frame,再按真实 glyph width 选择 `prefix...tail.ext`。这关闭了旧 visible-char/name-char budget 对 `editor base.zui`、`folder-open-line.svg` 这类标签的同字符数错误处理,同时保留扩展名扫描能力。

**Runtime surface measure-cache consumer note(2026-07-04)**:同一“字符左右间距/位置漂移”问题也可能来自 runtime surface 渲染重建阶段绕过缓存或缓存 key 太粗。`UiSurface` 现在持有跨 rebuild 的 `UiTextMeasureCache`,`ui/surface/render/extract.rs` 与 `text_fields.rs` 通过同一 helper 消费缓存；`UiTextMeasureKey` 同步纳入 owner frame 与 clip frame,避免同一文字/样式/宽度在不同绝对位置复用旧 `UiResolvedTextLine.frame`。这关闭的是 production render extract consumer 和绝对行框复用风险,不声称完成 09 的全局缓存/并行化或 live editor-window QA。

**Runtime frame-dedup production routing(2026-07-06)**:`UiTextMeasureCache` 现在持有 natural-size 与 full-layout 两张 `TextFrameDedup` 表,并在 persistent `TextMeasureCache`/`TextLayoutCache` 前按 exact key + exact text 复用同一帧重复请求。`resolve_or_shape(...)` 返回 owned `UiTextLayoutResolution`,render extract 直接消费缓存适配器结果,不再在提取路径额外 clone 借用值。该切片关闭的是 generic frame_dedup data-plane 到 production UI adapter 的重复请求接线；measure 闭包与 full layout 共享同一 `ShapedRunCache`、计数式 perf test 与 async/cache atlas 后续仍 open。验证日志写在 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_frame_dedup_production_routing_cargo_check_20260706.log`,不写 `target`。

**Runtime IME render-extract geometry note(2026-07-04)**:同一类“字形/间距已改善但光标或候选锚点看起来偏左偏右”的问题还会出现在 IME 几何使用另一套 layout 的时候。`ui/surface/input/editable_text/ime_context.rs` 现在在可刷新输入法上下文的事件上先刷新当前 tree 的 render extract,再从 matching `UiRenderCommand.text_layout` 读取 TextField 的实际 paint layout 来生成 cursor/composition rect；旧 fixed-column geometry 只作为 no-layout fallback。

**Runtime Dialog action runtime-measure note(2026-07-04)**:runtime surface Dialog action 仍保留 `text.chars().count() * DIALOG_ACTION_CHAR_WIDTH + 20` 的槽宽估算,会让同字符数 `iiii...` 与 `WWWW...` 分到同样 action frame。`ui/surface/render/dialog.rs` 现在用 runtime `measure_text_size(...)` 加显式 horizontal padding 与 min-width clamp 计算 action width,和 editor retained-host dialog action 修复保持同一“度量=绘制”方向。

**Editor diagnostics/text-marker runtime-measure note(2026-07-04)**:同一类字符左右留白问题还残留在 retained-host 诊断浮层和 text marker 画条上。`paint_diagnostics/marker.rs` 现在用 `measure_runtime_text_width(...)` + 既有 padding/clip 计算 debug-refresh marker frame；`paint_primitives/text_markers.rs` 用同一 helper 计算 text-bar frame,不再把同字符数的 `iiii...` 与 `WWWW...` 分配为同宽 overlay。该切片只收束 editor diagnostics/text-marker owner,不声称关闭真实窗口级 typography QA。

## text/index.md 现状评审已成立能力明细

### 2.1 已成立(取其能力,不推倒)

| 能力 | 落点 | 成熟度 |

| fontsdf SDF 烘焙 + 图集 + 渲染 | `ui/sdf_font_bake.rs`、`ui/sdf_atlas.rs`(LRU 256 槽)、`ui/sdf_render.rs`(R8Unorm)、`shaders/sdf_text.wgsl` | 单通道 SDF 全链可用;固定 bake/cache key、screenPxRange、shared atlas page identity/shelf/dirty/upload、page-keyed dirty/upload report、renderer texture-array layer consumption、shelf overflow 多页 slot allocation、page residency/LRU 决策与 rebuilt-page full-dirty invalidation 已首段落地,alpha/glyphon atlas 迁移与 MSDF 仍 pending |

| 字体回退链(硬编码) | `ui/text/font_registry.rs`(Inter/Noto/雅黑/Segoe) | 非脚本感知、非动态 |
| 富文本最小集 | `ui/text/rich_text.rs`(`**bold**`/`*italic*`/`` `code` ``) | 仅 markdown 三标记,无 HTML/BBCode |
| 字体资产/导入 | `asset/assets/font.rs`、`importer/ingest/import_font_asset/{mod.rs,parse_sfnt.rs}` | FR-M2 首段已具备 face_index/family members/变量实例/parsed metadata 与 sfnt/TTC 元数据解析;WOFF2 decode、真实 TTC/变量 fixture 与 SDF 多 face 渲染仍未闭合 |
| IME 上下文/编辑链 | `ui/surface/input/editable_text/ime_context.rs`、`ui/text/geometry.rs`、`edit_state.rs` | 出入站事件齐备,光标/合成 rect 已首段接入 resolved layout geometry;2026-07-04 IME update 正常路径改为刷新并消费 `render_extract` 中 paint 已用的 TextField layout,layout 不可用时才保留旧 fixed-column fallback |
| 渲染 DTO | `iface/ui/surface/render/`(`UiShapedGlyph`/`UiShapedText`/`UiTextPaint*`) | DTO 在,布局未喂真实数据 |

**Native bitmap atlas slot invalidation state(2026-07-07)**:主 native bitmap atlas 路径现在不再每帧只从空 `GlyphAtlasSet` 起步。`ScreenSpaceUiTextBackend` 在非空 native bitmap frame 间保留主 submission atlas,bitmap run/render submission/retry driver 均可接收上一帧 atlas,未引用 page 重建时记录 `GlyphAtlasBitmapSlotInvalidation`、递增 generation 并整页标脏。这让 page-generation/face-validity upload guard 能消费真实跨帧 atlas state,防止旧 slot 或 stale upload 静默写入；mixed-storage pass 的 persistent atlas 缺口由下一条 follow-up 关闭,true async raster worker、完整 glyph slot owner、focused Cargo green、live editor-window typography QA 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Native bitmap atlas mixed-storage persistent atlas(2026-07-07)**:`scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas/storage.rs` 现在承接 storage partition/submission owner,`NativeBitmapAtlasFrame::storage_submissions()` 把主 frame `self.submission.run.atlas.clone()` 传给每个 R8/RGBA storage submission,因此 mixed-storage replacement 不再在 storage split 后回到 default atlas。父 `native_bitmap_atlas.rs` 收束到 640 行,storage child 为 155 行；production `cargo check` 通过,focused Cargo test 在 Windows lib-test 编译/链接阶段超时未计 green。验证图 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_mixed_storage_persistent_preview_20260707.png` SHA256 `A4E5069E35EE7EC1B8279751C5D0334F102BC87A760CD9C86C42ED176D82F8F9`,target/cargo-target 同名扫描为 0。true async raster worker、完整 glyph slot owner/reuse、live editor-window typography QA 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Native bitmap atlas missing-raster-image fail-closed(2026-07-07)**:针对小字号编辑器标签仍可能出现的局部文字不适,native bitmap atlas 现在在 swash/source-cache 无法返回某个 glyph raster image 时记录 `missing_raster_image_count`,并通过 `NativeBitmapAtlasGlyphonFallbackReason::MissingRasterImage` 强制 glyphon fallback。这样 atlas 输入不完整时不会再只跳过该 glyph 后仍宣称替代 glyphon,避免 partial/stale native atlas 接管编辑器文本。focused `native_bitmap_atlas_frame_keeps_glyphon_when_raster_image_is_missing` 通过 1/1(7186 filtered),production `cargo check` 通过；验证图 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_atlas_missing_raster_image_fail_closed_preview_20260707.png` SHA256 `D0B56AFE9B985C7458761071DF19024D1F549CE6DEE7ADCDFDE260E571315AF5`,target/cargo-target 同名扫描为 0。true async raster worker、占位/近似桶首帧降级、live editor-window typography QA 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Runtime text async raster worker queue(2026-07-07)**:针对“等线已生效但小字号 label 字符左右间距/落点仍不舒服”的后续底层路径,本轮先把 PF-M2/PF-M3 的真实 swash raster work queue 落到 runtime text owner。`graphics/text/parallel/raster_pool.rs` 让 worker 线程只做 CPU glyph bitmap raster,每个 worker 独立持有 `SwashRasterizer`,completion 带 `page_generation` 与 `face_epoch`,由主线程 drain 决定 accepted/stale-page/face-invalidated。这样后续 native bitmap atlas 不再需要在 miss 路径里同步近似生成或静默跳过,可以接真实异步 glyph bitmap 结果。focused Cargo `text_raster_worker_pool` 已通过 5/5(7206 filtered),日志 SHA256 `897F3AF35147A0FCF27B1E3474D871E3C6B3CEDFCCD56DFFE442DAE0FB35556B`;该切片不是 live editor-window 最终验收,也未生成新 PNG。

**Runtime text parallel shape pool(2026-07-08)**:PF-M2 shaping 侧现在有 folder-backed data-plane owner。`graphics/text/parallel/shape_pool.rs` 接收 owned paragraph batch,用 `ShapedRunCacheKey + exact text` 先查/去重 `ShapedRunCache`,再只把唯一未命中 paragraph 交给 `TaskPool`/`parallel_for` 的 worker chunk,最后按请求原序返回 `Arc<ShapedGlyphRun>`。`render_perf_text_parallel_shape_count` 覆盖首批 3 个唯一 editor label shape、第二批 2 个 cache hit + 1 个新 miss;同批重复 miss 回归确保本帧重复段落只 shape 一次。静态验证日志为 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_parallel_shape_pool_*_20260708.*`,target/cargo-target PNG scan 为 0;同日 follow-up 为 `pending` 队列补 `Vec<PendingShapeJob>` 显式类型并复跑 focused Cargo `render_perf_text_parallel_shape_count` 1/1 passed。本切片非视觉,UI cache 显式 prewarm 入口由下方 follow-up 关闭;surface owner-text 自动收集由再下一条关闭。scroll raster/upload counters、live editor-window typography QA 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Runtime text UI paragraph shape-pool prewarm(2026-07-08)**:继续关闭 PF-M2 与用户 editor label 间距问题相关的生产接线首段。`UiTextShapePrewarmRequest` 与 `UiTextMeasureCache::prewarm_horizontal_paragraphs(...)` 把可见 UI row batch 送入同一个 `shape_paragraphs_with_cache(...)` 数据面,预热 key 使用 `UiTextDirection::Auto` 和完整 source range,与 natural-size/full-layout 的 shaped-run key 对齐。`render_perf_text_parallel_shape_pool_prewarms_ui_measure_cache` 覆盖 editor-style labels 和同批重复 row:prewarm 只 shape/insert 唯一 row,后续布局只增加 shaped hits,不增加 shaped miss/insert。Focused Cargo passed 1/1,日志 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_parallel_shape_pool_ui_prewarm_cargo_final_20260708.log` SHA256 `EE1284083D8D0A0EEA123EAC87A479D1E8EC9E91F0DE3F2F8820D38FEDDE7275`;target/cargo-target 同名 PNG scan 为 0。该切片非视觉,不生成 PNG;surface owner-text 自动 paragraph collection 由下一条关闭,live editor-window typography QA、scroll raster/upload counters 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Runtime text surface owner-text automatic prewarm(2026-07-08)**:`ui/surface/render/text_prewarm.rs` 现在在 render extract 生成 command 前自动收集当前 arranged draw order 内的可见 owner text,跳过 hidden 与组件 painter suppress,并通过 render-local `TaskPool` 预热到 `UiTextMeasureCache` 的 shared `ShapedRunCache`。`frame_shape_prewarm_report()` 让本帧 telemetry 可被测试断言。`render_extract_automatically_prewarms_visible_owner_text_before_layout` 覆盖 `editor base.zui` / `folder-open-outline.svg` 这类 owner labels:自动 prewarm requested 3、miss/insert 2、同批 duplicate 1,后续 layout 命中 shaped cache。Focused Cargo passed 1/1,日志 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_surface_auto_shape_prewarm_cargo_final_20260708.log` SHA256 `7AF294DB8A34FE868E3754C7E297FCE0884396CB4D6B3A95FDF507793453CCF2`;本切片非视觉,不生成 PNG。组件 painter 生成文本由下一条关闭;rich/vertical prewarm 已由后续 `from_layout_source(...)` 补记关闭,live editor-window typography QA、scroll raster/upload counters 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Runtime text surface component Text command prewarm/layout(2026-07-08)**:`ui/surface/render/text_prewarm.rs` 现在还在组件 painter commands 生成后收集仍缺 `text_layout` 的 `Text` command,先通过 `prewarm_render_command_text(...)` 把文本送入 shared `ShapedRunCache`,再通过 `resolve_missing_render_command_text_layouts(...)` 给命令补齐 resolved layout。`UiTextMeasureCache::prewarm_horizontal_paragraphs(...)` 的 frame report 改为累计多次 prewarm,避免 owner-text 与 command-text 批次互相覆盖。`render_extract_prewarms_and_layouts_component_text_commands` 覆盖 Button 组件生成的 `editor base.zui` / `folder-open-outline.svg` 文本:3 个 visible text commands 全部拥有 `text_layout`,prewarm requested 3、miss/insert 2、duplicate 1。Focused Cargo `prewarms` passed 3/3,日志 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_component_command_prewarm_cargo_retry_20260708.log` SHA256 `7020C78EAD179467B661D383928016CB2D90B2386E43930D31271861BF2E72E3`;本切片非视觉,不生成 PNG。rich/vertical prewarm 已由下一条关闭,live editor-window typography QA、scroll raster/upload counters 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Runtime text rich/vertical shape prewarm(2026-07-08)**:`UiTextShapePrewarmRequest::from_layout_source(...)` 现在是 owner-text 与 command-text prewarm 的统一 source 入口。rich text 通过 `parse_source_runs(text, true)` 投影为可见 layout text,避免 markup token 污染 shaped-run cache key；vertical writing mode 保留原 style 并依赖 `UiTextStyleKey` 的 writing-mode 维度隔离缓存。`prewarm_render_command_text_accepts_rich_and_vertical_commands` 与 `render_extract_prewarms_rich_and_vertical_owner_text_before_layout` focused Cargo 2/2 passed,日志 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_rich_vertical_prewarm_focused_cargo_20260708.log`;本切片非视觉,不生成 PNG。scroll raster/upload counters、per-page upload merge、live editor-window typography QA 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Runtime text worker completion bridge(2026-07-07)**:继续补 PF-M3 的 atlas 回流侧。`TextRasterCompletionDrain` 现在保留 stale page 与 face-invalidated completion 的 work id,让主线程不仅能计数也能清理 pending work；`scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas/source_cache.rs` 新增 worker request registration 与 completion-drain 应用入口,将 accepted `GlyphBitmap` 转成 native source cache 使用的 `SwashContent`/bearing/size/bytes,并把 failed、unknown、invalid bitmap、stale-page、face-invalidated 与 pending worker 计数写入 source-cache frame report。idle frame 与 face invalidation 会同时清空 pending worker key,避免旧异步结果悬挂。直接运行已生成 runtime lib-test binary 通过 `native_bitmap_atlas_source_cache` 5/5 与 worker drain 1/1,日志 SHA256 `C4E26C1EF98E95BBDA75609F7925CC658D19052A8E4F3DC0FCD187AB15B770F5` / `71FE22F34EA45DD9AAE6390131E1909AA4BFC7284903F1D2A4CB3020F63584FF`;target/cargo-target worker/source-cache PNG scan 为 0(SHA256 `C77FEC3A1A2BED814E3EC387015923C6DA97DCD71A80BE15E75E6DD1178CD64E`)。本切片不生成视觉图,仍未把 production native bitmap atlas miss scheduling、已闭合的 `SwashRasterRequest::glyphon_cache_key(...)` 接入真实 worker request、per-page upload merge、live editor-window typography QA 或完整 `TextAtlas` cutover 标为完成。

**Runtime text swash CacheKey request parity(2026-07-07)**:继续补 PF-M3 worker request 的底层语义。`graphics/text/raster/swash/request.rs` 现在能从 glyphon `CacheKey` 构造 `SwashRasterRequest`,保留 x/y offset、hint flags、pixel-font offset、fake italic、font weight variation 与 glyphon source fallback 顺序；`rasterizer.rs` 把 offset、transform、format、source list 与 `wght` variation 转交给 swash,并在 invalid offset 时 fail-closed。该切片不生成视觉图,因为它只修非视觉 request/rasterizer 数据面；后续仍需把 production native atlas miss scheduling 接到这一路 request、合并 per-page upload 并做 live editor-window typography QA。

**Runtime text production miss worker request scheduling(2026-07-07)**:继续把上面的 worker completion 与 glyphon CacheKey parity 接入生产 native bitmap atlas miss 路径。`ScreenSpaceUiTextBackend` 现在持有 optional `TextRasterWorkerPool`;native frame 先 drain completion,miss 时由 `NativeBitmapAtlasSourceCache::request_worker_image(...)` 用真实 font face index/font bytes 和 glyphon `CacheKey` 排队 `TextRasterWorkItem`,并以 `CacheKey` pending map 防重复提交。source image 只读取已完成 cache,所以当前帧仍 fail-closed 到 glyphon fallback,下一帧才能消费 worker result；`page_generation=0` 仍是 per-page upload merge 前的临时 target。focused lib test 通过 1/1(7258 filtered),日志 SHA256 `609DAB916950E0DACF5FDDEBE32426A2454DCE8844229B5DF12D6324DE8445BC`;宽 Cargo wrapper 因 unrelated incremental cgu object cache error 退出 101,日志 SHA256 `8A94A88A4216168A33632131E1D418E6CC24660C90ED8317D9D9967261AAFF81`;target/cargo-target 同名 PNG scan 为 0,SHA256 `EC2BCB08A6452B3A5CAF4FF6C384B66F9FC60AE01F36273A7061A0B9028FF1F0`。本切片非视觉,未生成 PNG；per-page upload merge、live editor-window typography QA 与完整 `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Editor retained-host nearest-phase quantization(2026-07-07)**:继续按用户最新 crop 优化 `editor base.zui` / `folder-op...line.svg` 的字形左右落点。7 月 6 日的 high-phase in-pixel clamp 可避免跨像素跳格,但会把 `20.95px`、`44.95px` 这类高相位系统性拉回 `7/8` bin,留下可见左/右偏置。当前 `paint_text/draw/placement/metrics.rs` 先把完整 screen x 四舍五入到 retained 1/8px 网格,再拆分 pixel 与 bin;`20.95px` 因此变为 `21px + 0/8`,最大量化误差回到 `0.0625px`。该策略不新增 letter-spacing、控件局部字体、ZUI token、root painter、runtime FontDatabase 或 atlas route。验证证据写入 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_phase_quantization_*`;本轮 recheck wrapper harness 通过 6/6,真实 retained proof PNG 在 `docs/tests/runtime/text`,target/cargo-target 同名 PNG 扫描为 0。更宽 Cargo screenshot rerun 在 Windows 编译 904s 后超时,没有 `test result` 或新 recheck PNG,不声明 package Cargo green。

**Editor retained-host grayscale device-glyph seed-visibility closeout(2026-07-08)**:针对用户截图中 DengXian/等线已生效但灰度小字号 glyph 左右落点仍不舒服的问题,默认 `HostTextSmoothing::Grayscale` 已收敛到 nearest device-pixel per-glyph placement,explicit Subpixel 仍保留完整 1/8px phase。复验时发现旧 Cargo 阻塞不是文本方案本身,而是 runtime prepare 中性材质捕获 DTO 仍被 test-only 门挡住;本轮按 `engine-code-structure-convention.md` / review findings 要求把 `MaterialCaptureSeed`、`MaterialRuntime::capture_seed()` 与 `resource_streamer_accessors/material_capture.rs` 迁出 test-only,但仍保持 crate-private 并只通过 `RuntimePrepareCollectorContext` 暴露中性 `RuntimePrepareMaterialCaptureSeed` / `sample_texture_rgba(...)`,没有公开 `ResourceStreamer`、`MaterialRuntime` 或 scene-resource internals。修复后新生成的 editor test binary 直接运行 `runtime_phase_guard_uses_device_pixel_bins_for_grayscale_glyphs` 1/1 通过、`grayscale` 7/7 通过、`retained_text_editor_crop_labels_keep_stable_ink_spacing` 导出复验 1/1 通过;新 proof PNG 位于 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_retained_grayscale_device_glyph_snap_seed_visibility_fix_20260708*`,full/narrow zoom4 已目检,同 stem target/cargo-target PNG 扫描为 0。Cargo wrapper 本次已越过旧 `RuntimePrepareMaterialCaptureSeed` import 阻塞并持续编译到工具 904s 超时,不声明 wrapper green;live editor-window typography QA 仍 open。

**Editor retained-host grayscale line-snap subpixel glyph phase(2026-07-08)**:用户最新局部截图继续指出“字体已是等线,但字符左右间距看起来很奇怪”。复查确认上面的 per-glyph device-pixel placement 会把自然 fractional advances 变成逐字整数像素步进,因此该策略现在作为历史尝试保留。当前有效规则是 default Grayscale 只 snap line origin,每个 glyph 仍保留 retained 1/8px phase；explicit Subpixel 保留 fractional line origin + 1/8px glyph phase。该修复仍在 retained-host text placement/layout owner 内,不新增 letter-spacing、控件局部字体、ZUI token、root painter、runtime FontDatabase 或 atlas route。fresh proof 通过 focused Cargo 并写入 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_grayscale_line_snap_subpixel_glyph_phase_20260708*`;同名误写到 `zircon_editor/docs/tests/runtime/text` 的文件已清理,target/cargo-target 同 stem scan 为 0。

**Runtime text writing-mode layout cache key(2026-07-08)**:`UiTextStyleKey` 现在记录 `UiTextWritingMode`,防止 HorizontalTb 与 VerticalRl 在相同 text/frame/style 其它字段下复用 full-layout cache 或 same-frame dedup。该修复关闭 LB-M4/PF-M1 缓存键缺口,但不声明完整竖排 source-range geometry 或 live editor-window typography QA 完成；rich/vertical prewarm 已由 09 PF-M2 follow-up 关闭。

**Runtime native atlas transparent placeholder for worker-pending glyphs(2026-07-07)**:PF-M3 production miss scheduling 后,worker-pending glyph 不再让本帧回到 glyphon fallback。`native_bitmap_atlas.rs` 对已经 pending 或刚提交的 worker miss 追加 `GlyphAtlasBitmapPlaceholderMode::TransparentQuad`;`render_submission/plan.rs` 支持在 retry-frame submission 后追加 placeholder draw plan;`native_bitmap_atlas/handoff.rs` 把该状态投影为 `NativeBitmapAtlasHandoff::TransparentPlaceholder`,root `scene_renderer/ui/text.rs` 走真实 native atlas renderer prepare 分支并关闭 glyphon。这样首帧缺字保持透明占位且不发生 render path 抖动,下一帧由 worker completion/source cache 替换真实 glyph。Focused runtime tests `render_text_atlas_bitmap_submission_appends_worker_pending_placeholders`、`native_bitmap_atlas_frame_schedules_worker_miss_as_transparent_placeholder` 与 `native_bitmap_atlas_first_frame_degradation_reports_placeholder_work` 均通过 1/1,日志 SHA256 `2CD98E6D27157649062B106EF0D77AB3C8625D9009726BD8959F77118B7EB262` / `597021BF1AB905AEC5733FAA2E77242CC52CFCE358B50239B021D206C99E90B1` / `E1E2F44433689A234F5701D8B05AEFFA5D558D7D64CB2D467896A89643EE42BD`。该切片仍不完成 per-page upload merge、scroll raster/upload perf counters、live editor-window typography QA 或 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover。

**Runtime native atlas approximate bucket replacement(2026-07-07)**:继续关闭 PF-M3 “占位/已有近似桶”中的近似桶分支。`NativeBitmapAtlasSourceCache` 新增同 font/glyph/size/weight/flags、不同 subpixel bin 的保守近似查找与 `approximate_hit_count`;`native_bitmap_atlas.rs` 在 exact cache miss 时先排队 exact worker request,若相邻 bucket 已有图像则用它生成当前帧 source image,不再追加透明占位;`NativeBitmapAtlasPrepareReport.approximate_raster_image_count` 与 `NativeBitmapAtlasFirstFrameDegradation::ApproximateBucketReplacement` 让 telemetry 明确该帧是近似降级替代。该切片不改 glyph atlas public facade、字体族、ZUI asset、组件 letter-spacing、root painter 或 per-page upload merge。静态验证 `runtime_text_native_atlas_approx_bucket_static_check_20260707.log` 通过 rustfmt/diff/字段覆盖扫描,SHA256 `ADDE952DA2030E7AB8246E555A370814A31CA0E4454C181C27798D7B64FE826C`;Windows focused `--lib` 与 WSL `/tmp` target 均在编译阶段超时,日志 SHA256 `6E3EA51A1E2FAA3764DA86EC0295B0A6514C7068E20DAD6D7BBC7355215CAB61` / `CDBF68EA4358BD9212588FF74A379FBE660B0DBFDCE1BA46AF2C5E7C79CCA447`,所以不声明 focused Cargo green。本切片非视觉,未生成 PNG;per-page upload merge、scroll raster/upload perf counters、live editor-window typography QA 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Runtime native bitmap stable horizontal subpixel cache key(2026-07-08)**:`scene_renderer/ui/text/native_bitmap_atlas/source_cache.rs` 现在把 glyphon `CacheKey.x_bin` 归一为 `SubpixelBin::Zero`,并在 worker registration、cache lookup、approximate lookup、worker request、pending check 与 insert 入口统一使用该 stable key。这样 horizontal subpixel phase 不再为同一个 glyph 生成多份 source image 或重复 worker pending key；`y_bin` 保留给纵向邻桶近似,不改变 vertical fallback 语义。scoped rustfmt 通过,`cargo check -p zircon_runtime --lib --tests --no-default-features --locked --jobs 1` exit 0,日志 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_native_bitmap_stable_phase_check_tests_20260708.log` SHA256 `4C1B97B79C5783176B6C03256EDDF4D2B696FABA54D4E01CD730B6E169E4EE66`;完整 `native_bitmap_atlas` Cargo test 在工具窗口超时停止,不声明 full green。本切片非视觉,没有新增 PNG,视觉 proof 仍引用 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_editor_grayscale_line_snap_subpixel_glyph_phase_20260708*.png`;per-page upload merge、scroll raster/upload counters、live editor-window typography QA 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。

**Runtime text scroll cache reuse shape/layout guard(2026-07-07)**:继续关闭 PF-M4 滚动列表性能计数首段。`ui/tests/text_pipeline.rs` 新增 `render_perf_text_scroll_list_reuses_cache`:预热 `"Hg"` metrics 后先 layout 5 行编辑器式资源 label,再滚动 3 行；第二帧 `ShapedRunCache` 只能为 3 个新 row 产生 miss/insert,2 个重叠 row 必须 hit,同时 absolute layout 因 y 变化仍 miss,防止把旧几何误复用到新行位。该切片不改字体族、letter-spacing、ZUI token、root painter、atlas handoff 或 raster worker。静态验证日志位于 `docs/tests/runtime/text/runtime_text_scroll_cache_reuse_perf_*_20260707.log`:rustfmt SHA256 `E3B0C44298FC1C149AFBF4C8996FB92427AE41E4649B934CA495991B7852B855`,diff-check SHA256 `22FD7413CC13074CC1E6687BCD6B088A4C9EA15A00B765AC4A5F1739E0495A05`; final scoped diff-check SHA256 `0C2C4EF8AAB3E3943C4674E009561D3F5E8FAB3429CC0B08C123E71ADF59EBC3`,target/cargo-target PNG scan 0 SHA256 `E99A2829ECABA7855E71B61F879991A86D2DF3070B1CD2A23AD2D4242C975B7D`,active lanes SHA256 `FF8B1878275A1D2AA7843370F3664834CF45966B479F68615D6BA4A18FF93D2D`;focused Cargo 因外部编译通道活跃 deferred。本切片非视觉 perf guard,未生成 PNG;raster/upload bytes 与 cache hit-rate counters、parallel shape count、live editor-window typography QA 与 full glyphon `TextAtlas` cutover 仍 open。
